+        const Material* materials = gpu.materials;
+        const val_t* read_energy = gpu.energy[read_buffer];
+        const val_t* read_total_flux = gpu.total_flux[read_buffer];
+        val_t* write_energy = gpu.energy[write_buffer];
+        val_t* write_total_flux = gpu.total_flux[write_buffer];
+        void* kernel_args[] = {
+            &n_elems, &material_indices, &connection_counts, &connection_indices,
+            &connection_fluxes, &materials, &read_energy, &read_total_flux,
+            &write_energy, &write_total_flux
+        };
+
+        cudaKernelNodeParams kernel_params{};
+        kernel_params.func = reinterpret_cast<void*>(updateElementsKernel);
+        kernel_params.gridDim = dim3(static_cast<unsigned int>(
+            (gpu.n_elems + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE));
+        kernel_params.blockDim = dim3(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE);
+        kernel_params.kernelParams = kernel_args;
+
+        cudaGraphNode_t current_node = nullptr;
+        if (previous_node == nullptr) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphAddKernelNode(&current_node, gpu.graph, nullptr, 0,
+                                              &kernel_params));
+        } else {

+                                              &kernel_params));

-        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);

     }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&gpu.graph_exec, gpu.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
+    gpu.final_buffer = n_iters & 1;
 }
 
+GpuSimulation uploadSimulation(const World& world, int n_iters) {
+    GpuSimulation gpu;
+    gpu.n_elems = world.elements_static.size();
+    if (gpu.n_elems == 0) {
+        buildExecutionGraph(gpu, n_iters);
+        return gpu;
+    }
+
+    std::vector<uint8_t> material_indices(gpu.n_elems);
+    std::vector<uint8_t> connection_counts(gpu.n_elems);
+    std::vector<uint32_t> connection_indices(gpu.n_elems * MAX_CONNECTIONS);
+    std::vector<val_t> connection_fluxes(gpu.n_elems * MAX_CONNECTIONS);
+    std::vector<val_t> initial_energy(gpu.n_elems);
+    std::vector<val_t> initial_total_flux(gpu.n_elems);
+
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < gpu.n_elems; ++i) {
+        const ElementStatic& source_static = world.elements_static[i];
+        const ElementDynamic& source_dynamic = world.elements_dynamic[i];
+        material_indices[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(source_static.material_idx);
+        connection_counts[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(source_static.num_connections);
+        initial_energy[i] = source_dynamic.current_energy;
+        initial_total_flux[i] = source_dynamic.total_flux;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {
+            const size_t connection_offset = j * gpu.n_elems + i;
+            connection_indices[connection_offset] =
+                static_cast<uint32_t>(source_static.connected_idx[j]);
+            connection_fluxes[connection_offset] = source_static.connected_flux[j];
+        }
+    }
+
+    allocateDevice(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&gpu.material_indices),
+                   gpu.n_elems * sizeof(*gpu.material_indices));
+    allocateDevice(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&gpu.connection_counts),
+                   gpu.n_elems * sizeof(*gpu.connection_counts));
+    allocateDevice(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&gpu.connection_indices),
+                   connection_indices.size() * sizeof(*gpu.connection_indices));
+    allocateDevice(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&gpu.connection_fluxes),
+                   connection_fluxes.size() * sizeof(*gpu.connection_fluxes));
+    allocateDevice(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&gpu.materials),
+                   world.materials.size() * sizeof(*gpu.materials));
+    for (int buffer = 0; buffer < 2; ++buffer) {
+        allocateDevice(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&gpu.energy[buffer]),
+                       gpu.n_elems * sizeof(*gpu.energy[buffer]));
+        allocateDevice(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&gpu.total_flux[buffer]),
+                       gpu.n_elems * sizeof(*gpu.total_flux[buffer]));
+    }
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(gpu.material_indices, material_indices.data(),
+                          material_indices.size() * sizeof(*gpu.material_indices),
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(gpu.connection_counts, connection_counts.data(),
+                          connection_counts.size() * sizeof(*gpu.connection_counts),
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(gpu.connection_indices, connection_indices.data(),
+                          connection_indices.size() * sizeof(*gpu.connection_indices),
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(gpu.connection_fluxes, connection_fluxes.data(),
+                          connection_fluxes.size() * sizeof(*gpu.connection_fluxes),
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(gpu.materials, world.materials.data(),
+                          world.materials.size() * sizeof(*gpu.materials),
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(gpu.energy[0], initial_energy.data(),
+                          initial_energy.size() * sizeof(*gpu.energy[0]),
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(gpu.total_flux[0], initial_total_flux.data(),
+                          initial_total_flux.size() * sizeof(*gpu.total_flux[0]),
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+
+    buildExecutionGraph(gpu, n_iters);
+    return gpu;
+}
+
+void runSimulation(GpuSimulation& gpu) {
+    if (gpu.graph_exec != nullptr) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(gpu.graph_exec, nullptr));
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+}
+
+void downloadSimulation(World& world, const GpuSimulation& gpu) {
+    if (gpu.n_elems == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    char* energy_destination = reinterpret_cast<char*>(world.elements_dynamic.data()) +
+                               offsetof(ElementDynamic, current_energy);
+    char* flux_destination = reinterpret_cast<char*>(world.elements_dynamic.data()) +
+                             offsetof(ElementDynamic, total_flux);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(energy_destination, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+                            gpu.energy[gpu.final_buffer], sizeof(val_t), sizeof(val_t), gpu.n_elems,
+                            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(flux_destination, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+                            gpu.total_flux[gpu.final_buffer], sizeof(val_t), sizeof(val_t), gpu.n_elems,
+                            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+}
+
+void releaseSimulation(GpuSimulation& gpu) {
+    if (gpu.graph_exec != nullptr) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphExecDestroy(gpu.graph_exec));
+    }
+    if (gpu.graph != nullptr) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphDestroy(gpu.graph));
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.material_indices));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.connection_counts));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.connection_indices));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.connection_fluxes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.materials));
+    for (int buffer = 0; buffer < 2; ++buffer) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.energy[buffer]));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.total_flux[buffer]));
+    }
+}
+
+}  // namespace
+

+namespace {
+
+constexpr unsigned int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaCheckFailed(cudaError_t status, const char* expression,
+                                  const char* file, int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while executing %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            expression, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression)                                                      \
+    do {                                                                            \
+        const cudaError_t cuda_status_ = (expression);                              \
+        if (cuda_status_ != cudaSuccess) {                                          \
+            cudaCheckFailed(cuda_status_, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__);         \
+        }                                                                           \
+    } while (false)
+
+// Metadata is transposed before upload: connection k for adjacent elements is
+// adjacent in memory.  This keeps every connection pass through a warp fully
+// coalesced, while retaining the original arbitrary adjacency-list semantics.
+// The mesh builder uses int element indices and the three fixed material IDs,
+// so their compact device representations are lossless for every valid input.
+__global__ void updateElementsKernel(
+    size_t n_elems,
+    const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_indices,
+    const uint8_t* __restrict__ connection_counts,
+    const uint32_t* __restrict__ connection_indices,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ connection_fluxes,
+    const Material* __restrict__ materials,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ read_energy,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ read_total_flux,
+    val_t* __restrict__ write_energy,
+    val_t* __restrict__ write_total_flux) {
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n_elems) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const Material material = materials[material_indices[i]];
+    const val_t current_energy = read_energy[i];
+    val_t total_flux = material.external_flow;
+    const uint8_t connection_count = connection_counts[i];
+
+    // MAX_CONNECTIONS is a compile-time constant.  Predicated, unrolled loads
+    // avoid loop-control overhead without accessing inactive graph entries.
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {
+        if (j < connection_count) {
+            const size_t connection_offset = static_cast<size_t>(j) * n_elems + i;
+            const uint32_t neighbor_idx = connection_indices[connection_offset];
+            const val_t neighbor_energy = read_energy[neighbor_idx];
+            total_flux += (neighbor_energy - current_energy) * material.transfer_coeff *
+                          connection_fluxes[connection_offset] * 0.25;
+        }
+    }
+
+    write_energy[i] = current_energy + total_flux;
+    write_total_flux[i] = read_total_flux[i] + fabs(total_flux);
+}
+

+    Material* materials = nullptr;
+    val_t* energy[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    val_t* total_flux[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t graph_exec = nullptr;
+    int final_buffer = 0;
+};

+void allocateDevice(void** pointer, size_t bytes) {
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(pointer, bytes));

+    int device_count = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count));
+    if (device_count == 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error: no CUDA-capable device is available\n");
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(0));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr));  // Create the context before benchmark timing.
+}

+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));

+    buildExecutionGraph(gpu, n_iters);
+    return gpu;
+}
+
+void runSimulation(GpuSimulation& gpu) {
+    if (gpu.graph_exec != nullptr) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(gpu.graph_exec, nullptr));
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+}
+
+void downloadSimulation(World& world, const GpuSimulation& gpu) {
+    if (gpu.n_elems == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    char* energy_destination = reinterpret_cast<char*>(world.elements_dynamic.data()) +
+                               offsetof(ElementDynamic, current_energy);
+    char* flux_destination = reinterpret_cast<char*>(world.elements_dynamic.data()) +
+                             offsetof(ElementDynamic, total_flux);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(energy_destination, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+                            gpu.energy[gpu.final_buffer], sizeof(val_t), sizeof(val_t), gpu.n_elems,
+                            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(flux_destination, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+                            gpu.total_flux[gpu.final_buffer], sizeof(val_t), sizeof(val_t), gpu.n_elems,
+                            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+}
+
+void releaseSimulation(GpuSimulation& gpu) {
+    if (gpu.graph_exec != nullptr) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphExecDestroy(gpu.graph_exec));
+    }
+    if (gpu.graph != nullptr) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphDestroy(gpu.graph));
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.material_indices));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.connection_counts));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.connection_indices));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.connection_fluxes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.materials));
+    for (int buffer = 0; buffer < 2; ++buffer) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.energy[buffer]));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.total_flux[buffer]));
+    }
+}
+

   111	// Compute energy flux between two elements
   112	inline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,
   113	                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {
   114	    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * 
   115	           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;
   116	}

   118	namespace {

   120	constexpr unsigned int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
   121	
   122	[[noreturn]] void cudaCheckFailed(cudaError_t status, const char* expression,
   123	                                  const char* file, int line) {
   124	    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while executing %s: %s\n", file, line,
   125	            expression, cudaGetErrorString(status));
   126	    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
   127	}
   128	
   129	#define CUDA_CHECK(expression)                                                      \
   130	    do {                                                                            \
   131	        const cudaError_t cuda_status_ = (expression);                              \
   132	        if (cuda_status_ != cudaSuccess) {                                          \
   133	            cudaCheckFailed(cuda_status_, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__);         \
   134	        }                                                                           \
   135	    } while (false)
   136	
   137	// Metadata is transposed before upload: connection k for adjacent elements is
   138	// adjacent in memory.  This keeps every connection pass through a warp fully
   139	// coalesced, while retaining the original arbitrary adjacency-list semantics.
   140	// The mesh builder uses int element indices and the three fixed material IDs,
   141	// so their compact device representations are lossless for every valid input.
   142	__global__ void updateElementsKernel(
   143	    size_t n_elems,
   144	    const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_indices,
   145	    const uint8_t* __restrict__ connection_counts,
   146	    const uint32_t* __restrict__ connection_indices,
   147	    const val_t* __restrict__ connection_fluxes,
   148	    const Material* __restrict__ materials,
   149	    const val_t* __restrict__ read_energy,
   150	    const val_t* __restrict__ read_total_flux,
   151	    val_t* __restrict__ write_energy,
   152	    val_t* __restrict__ write_total_flux) {
   153	    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
   154	    if (i >= n_elems) {
   155	        return;
   156	    }
   157	
   158	    const Material material = materials[material_indices[i]];
   159	    const val_t current_energy = read_energy[i];
   160	    val_t total_flux = material.external_flow;
   161	    const uint8_t connection_count = connection_counts[i];
   162	
   163	    // MAX_CONNECTIONS is a compile-time constant.  Predicated, unrolled loads
   164	    // avoid loop-control overhead without accessing inactive graph entries.
   165	#pragma unroll
   166	    for (unsigned int j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {
   167	        if (j < connection_count) {
   168	            const size_t connection_offset = static_cast<size_t>(j) * n_elems + i;
   169	            const uint32_t neighbor_idx = connection_indices[connection_offset];
   170	            const val_t neighbor_energy = read_energy[neighbor_idx];
   171	            total_flux += (neighbor_energy - current_energy) * material.transfer_coeff *
   172	                          connection_fluxes[connection_offset] * 0.25;
   173	        }
   174	    }
   175	
   176	    write_energy[i] = current_energy + total_flux;
   177	    write_total_flux[i] = read_total_flux[i] + fabs(total_flux);
   178	}
   179	
   180	struct GpuSimulation {
   181	    size_t n_elems = 0;
   182	    uint8_t* material_indices = nullptr;
   183	    uint8_t* connection_counts = nullptr;
   184	    uint32_t* connection_indices = nullptr;
   185	    val_t* connection_fluxes = nullptr;
   186	    Material* materials = nullptr;
   187	    val_t* energy[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
   188	    val_t* total_flux[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
   189	    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
   190	    cudaGraphExec_t graph_exec = nullptr;
   191	    int final_buffer = 0;
   192	};
   193	
   194	void allocateDevice(void** pointer, size_t bytes) {
   195	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(pointer, bytes));
   196	}
   197	
   198	void initializeCuda() {
   199	    int device_count = 0;
   200	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count));
   201	    if (device_count == 0) {
   202	        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error: no CUDA-capable device is available\n");
   203	        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
   204	    }
   205	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(0));
   206	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr));  // Create the context before benchmark timing.
   207	}
   208	
   209	void buildExecutionGraph(GpuSimulation& gpu, int n_iters) {
   210	    if (n_iters <= 0 || gpu.n_elems == 0) {
   211	        gpu.final_buffer = 0;
   212	        return;
   213	    }
   214	
   215	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphCreate(&gpu.graph, 0));
   216	    cudaGraphNode_t previous_node = nullptr;
   217	    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
   218	        const int read_buffer = iter & 1;
   219	        const int write_buffer = read_buffer ^ 1;
   220	        size_t n_elems = gpu.n_elems;
   221	        const uint8_t* material_indices = gpu.material_indices;
   222	        const uint8_t* connection_counts = gpu.connection_counts;
   223	        const uint32_t* connection_indices = gpu.connection_indices;
   224	        const val_t* connection_fluxes = gpu.connection_fluxes;
   225	        const Material* materials = gpu.materials;
   226	        const val_t* read_energy = gpu.energy[read_buffer];
   227	        const val_t* read_total_flux = gpu.total_flux[read_buffer];
   228	        val_t* write_energy = gpu.energy[write_buffer];
   229	        val_t* write_total_flux = gpu.total_flux[write_buffer];
   230	        void* kernel_args[] = {
   231	            &n_elems, &material_indices, &connection_counts, &connection_indices,
   232	            &connection_fluxes, &materials, &read_energy, &read_total_flux,
   233	            &write_energy, &write_total_flux
   234	        };
   235	
   236	        cudaKernelNodeParams kernel_params{};
   237	        kernel_params.func = reinterpret_cast<void*>(updateElementsKernel);
   238	        kernel_params.gridDim = dim3(static_cast<unsigned int>(
   239	            (gpu.n_elems + CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE));
   240	        kernel_params.blockDim = dim3(CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE);
   241	        kernel_params.kernelParams = kernel_args;
   242	
   243	        cudaGraphNode_t current_node = nullptr;
   244	        if (previous_node == nullptr) {
   245	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphAddKernelNode(&current_node, gpu.graph, nullptr, 0,
   246	                                              &kernel_params));
   247	        } else {
   248	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphAddKernelNode(&current_node, gpu.graph, &previous_node, 1,
   249	                                              &kernel_params));
   250	        }
   251	        previous_node = current_node;
   252	    }
   253	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphInstantiate(&gpu.graph_exec, gpu.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
   254	    gpu.final_buffer = n_iters & 1;
   255	}
   256	
   257	GpuSimulation uploadSimulation(const World& world, int n_iters) {
   258	    GpuSimulation gpu;
   259	    gpu.n_elems = world.elements_static.size();
   260	    if (gpu.n_elems == 0) {
   261	        buildExecutionGraph(gpu, n_iters);
   262	        return gpu;
   263	    }
   264	
   265	    std::vector<uint8_t> material_indices(gpu.n_elems);
   266	    std::vector<uint8_t> connection_counts(gpu.n_elems);
   267	    std::vector<uint32_t> connection_indices(gpu.n_elems * MAX_CONNECTIONS);
   268	    std::vector<val_t> connection_fluxes(gpu.n_elems * MAX_CONNECTIONS);
   269	    std::vector<val_t> initial_energy(gpu.n_elems);
   270	    std::vector<val_t> initial_total_flux(gpu.n_elems);
   271	
   272	    for (size_t i = 0; i < gpu.n_elems; ++i) {
   273	        const ElementStatic& source_static = world.elements_static[i];
   274	        const ElementDynamic& source_dynamic = world.elements_dynamic[i];
   275	        material_indices[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(source_static.material_idx);
   276	        connection_counts[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(source_static.num_connections);
   277	        initial_energy[i] = source_dynamic.current_energy;
   278	        initial_total_flux[i] = source_dynamic.total_flux;
   279	        for (size_t j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {
   280	            const size_t connection_offset = j * gpu.n_elems + i;
   281	            connection_indices[connection_offset] =
   282	                static_cast<uint32_t>(source_static.connected_idx[j]);
   283	            connection_fluxes[connection_offset] = source_static.connected_flux[j];
   284	        }
   285	    }
   286	
   287	    allocateDevice(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&gpu.material_indices),
   288	                   gpu.n_elems * sizeof(*gpu.material_indices));
   289	    allocateDevice(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&gpu.connection_counts),
   290	                   gpu.n_elems * sizeof(*gpu.connection_counts));
   291	    allocateDevice(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&gpu.connection_indices),
   292	                   connection_indices.size() * sizeof(*gpu.connection_indices));
   293	    allocateDevice(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&gpu.connection_fluxes),
   294	                   connection_fluxes.size() * sizeof(*gpu.connection_fluxes));
   295	    allocateDevice(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&gpu.materials),
   296	                   world.materials.size() * sizeof(*gpu.materials));
   297	    for (int buffer = 0; buffer < 2; ++buffer) {
   298	        allocateDevice(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&gpu.energy[buffer]),
   299	                       gpu.n_elems * sizeof(*gpu.energy[buffer]));
   300	        allocateDevice(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&gpu.total_flux[buffer]),
   301	                       gpu.n_elems * sizeof(*gpu.total_flux[buffer]));
   302	    }
   303	
   304	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(gpu.material_indices, material_indices.data(),
   305	                          material_indices.size() * sizeof(*gpu.material_indices),
   306	                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   307	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(gpu.connection_counts, connection_counts.data(),
   308	                          connection_counts.size() * sizeof(*gpu.connection_counts),
   309	                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   310	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(gpu.connection_indices, connection_indices.data(),
   311	                          connection_indices.size() * sizeof(*gpu.connection_indices),
   312	                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   313	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(gpu.connection_fluxes, connection_fluxes.data(),
   314	                          connection_fluxes.size() * sizeof(*gpu.connection_fluxes),
   315	                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   316	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(gpu.materials, world.materials.data(),
   317	                          world.materials.size() * sizeof(*gpu.materials),
   318	                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   319	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(gpu.energy[0], initial_energy.data(),
   320	                          initial_energy.size() * sizeof(*gpu.energy[0]),
   321	                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   322	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(gpu.total_flux[0], initial_total_flux.data(),
   323	                          initial_total_flux.size() * sizeof(*gpu.total_flux[0]),
   324	                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   325	
   326	    buildExecutionGraph(gpu, n_iters);
   327	    return gpu;
   328	}
   329

   331	    if (gpu.graph_exec != nullptr) {
   332	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(gpu.graph_exec, nullptr));
   333	    }
   334	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
   335	}

   343	                               offsetof(ElementDynamic, current_energy);

+[[noreturn]] void cudaCheckFailed(cudaError_t status, const char* expression,
+                                  const char* file, int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while executing %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            expression, cudaGetErrorString(status));

+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression)                                                      \
+    do {                                                                            \
+        const cudaError_t cuda_status_ = (expression);                              \
+        if (cuda_status_ != cudaSuccess) {                                          \
+            cudaCheckFailed(cuda_status_, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__);         \
+        }                                                                           \
+    } while (false)

+// Metadata is transposed before upload: connection k for adjacent elements is
+// adjacent in memory.  This keeps every connection pass through a warp fully
+// coalesced, while retaining the original arbitrary adjacency-list semantics.
+// The mesh builder uses int element indices and the three fixed material IDs,
+// so their compact device representations are lossless for every valid input.
+__global__ void updateElementsKernel(
+    size_t n_elems,
+    const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_indices,
+    const uint8_t* __restrict__ connection_counts,
+    const uint32_t* __restrict__ connection_indices,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ connection_fluxes,
+    const Material* __restrict__ materials,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ read_energy,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ read_total_flux,
+    val_t* __restrict__ write_energy,
+    val_t* __restrict__ write_total_flux) {
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n_elems) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const Material material = materials[material_indices[i]];
+    const val_t current_energy = read_energy[i];
+    val_t total_flux = material.external_flow;
+    const uint8_t connection_count = connection_counts[i];
+
+    // MAX_CONNECTIONS is a compile-time constant.  Predicated, unrolled loads
+    // avoid loop-control overhead without accessing inactive graph entries.
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {
+        if (j < connection_count) {
+            const size_t connection_offset = static_cast<size_t>(j) * n_elems + i;
+            const uint32_t neighbor_idx = connection_indices[connection_offset];
+            const val_t neighbor_energy = read_energy[neighbor_idx];
+            total_flux += (neighbor_energy - current_energy) * material.transfer_coeff *
+                          connection_fluxes[connection_offset] * 0.25;
+        }
+    }
+
+    write_energy[i] = current_energy + total_flux;
+    write_total_flux[i] = read_total_flux[i] + fabs(total_flux);
+}
+
+struct GpuSimulation {
+    size_t n_elems = 0;
+    uint8_t* material_indices = nullptr;
+    uint8_t* connection_counts = nullptr;
+    uint32_t* connection_indices = nullptr;
+    val_t* connection_fluxes = nullptr;
+    Material* materials = nullptr;
+    val_t* energy[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    val_t* total_flux[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t graph_exec = nullptr;
+    int final_buffer = 0;
+};
+
+void allocateDevice(void** pointer, size_t bytes) {
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(pointer, bytes));
+}

+void initializeCuda() {

+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count));
+    if (device_count == 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error: no CUDA-capable device is available\n");
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(0));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr));  // Create the context before benchmark timing.
+}

+        gpu.final_buffer = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+

+        val_t* write_total_flux = gpu.total_flux[write_buffer];
+        void* kernel_args[] = {
+            &n_elems, &material_indices, &connection_counts, &connection_indices,
+            &connection_fluxes, &materials, &read_energy, &read_total_flux,
+            &write_energy, &write_total_flux

+GpuSimulation uploadSimulation(const World& world, int n_iters) {
+    GpuSimulation gpu;
+    gpu.n_elems = world.elements_static.size();
+    if (gpu.n_elems == 0) {
+        buildExecutionGraph(gpu, n_iters);
+        return gpu;
+    }
+
+    std::vector<uint8_t> material_indices(gpu.n_elems);
+    std::vector<uint8_t> connection_counts(gpu.n_elems);
+    std::vector<uint32_t> connection_indices(gpu.n_elems * MAX_CONNECTIONS);
+    std::vector<val_t> connection_fluxes(gpu.n_elems * MAX_CONNECTIONS);
+    std::vector<val_t> initial_energy(gpu.n_elems);
+    std::vector<val_t> initial_total_flux(gpu.n_elems);
+
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < gpu.n_elems; ++i) {
+        const ElementStatic& source_static = world.elements_static[i];
+        const ElementDynamic& source_dynamic = world.elements_dynamic[i];
+        material_indices[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(source_static.material_idx);
+        connection_counts[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(source_static.num_connections);
+        initial_energy[i] = source_dynamic.current_energy;
+        initial_total_flux[i] = source_dynamic.total_flux;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {
+            const size_t connection_offset = j * gpu.n_elems + i;
+            connection_indices[connection_offset] =
+                static_cast<uint32_t>(source_static.connected_idx[j]);
+            connection_fluxes[connection_offset] = source_static.connected_flux[j];
+        }
+    }
+
+    allocateDevice(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&gpu.material_indices),
+                   gpu.n_elems * sizeof(*gpu.material_indices));
+    allocateDevice(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&gpu.connection_counts),
+                   gpu.n_elems * sizeof(*gpu.connection_counts));
+    allocateDevice(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&gpu.connection_indices),
+                   connection_indices.size() * sizeof(*gpu.connection_indices));
+    allocateDevice(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&gpu.connection_fluxes),
+                   connection_fluxes.size() * sizeof(*gpu.connection_fluxes));
+    allocateDevice(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&gpu.materials),
+                   world.materials.size() * sizeof(*gpu.materials));
+    for (int buffer = 0; buffer < 2; ++buffer) {
+        allocateDevice(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&gpu.energy[buffer]),
+                       gpu.n_elems * sizeof(*gpu.energy[buffer]));
+        allocateDevice(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&gpu.total_flux[buffer]),
+                       gpu.n_elems * sizeof(*gpu.total_flux[buffer]));
+    }
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(gpu.material_indices, material_indices.data(),
+                          material_indices.size() * sizeof(*gpu.material_indices),
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(gpu.connection_counts, connection_counts.data(),
+                          connection_counts.size() * sizeof(*gpu.connection_counts),
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(gpu.connection_indices, connection_indices.data(),
+                          connection_indices.size() * sizeof(*gpu.connection_indices),
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(gpu.connection_fluxes, connection_fluxes.data(),
+                          connection_fluxes.size() * sizeof(*gpu.connection_fluxes),
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(gpu.materials, world.materials.data(),
+                          world.materials.size() * sizeof(*gpu.materials),
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(gpu.energy[0], initial_energy.data(),
+                          initial_energy.size() * sizeof(*gpu.energy[0]),
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(gpu.total_flux[0], initial_total_flux.data(),
+                          initial_total_flux.size() * sizeof(*gpu.total_flux[0]),
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+
+    buildExecutionGraph(gpu, n_iters);
+    return gpu;
+}
+
+void runSimulation(GpuSimulation& gpu) {
+    if (gpu.graph_exec != nullptr) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(gpu.graph_exec, nullptr));
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+}
+
+void downloadSimulation(World& world, const GpuSimulation& gpu) {
+    if (gpu.n_elems == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    char* energy_destination = reinterpret_cast<char*>(world.elements_dynamic.data()) +
+                               offsetof(ElementDynamic, current_energy);
+    char* flux_destination = reinterpret_cast<char*>(world.elements_dynamic.data()) +
+                             offsetof(ElementDynamic, total_flux);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(energy_destination, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+                            gpu.energy[gpu.final_buffer], sizeof(val_t), sizeof(val_t), gpu.n_elems,
+                            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(flux_destination, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+                            gpu.total_flux[gpu.final_buffer], sizeof(val_t), sizeof(val_t), gpu.n_elems,
+                            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+}
+
+void releaseSimulation(GpuSimulation& gpu) {
+    if (gpu.graph_exec != nullptr) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphExecDestroy(gpu.graph_exec));
+    }
+    if (gpu.graph != nullptr) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphDestroy(gpu.graph));
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.material_indices));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.connection_counts));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.connection_indices));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.connection_fluxes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.materials));
+    for (int buffer = 0; buffer < 2; ++buffer) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.energy[buffer]));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.total_flux[buffer]));
+    }
+}
+
+}  // namespace

Implemented CUDA parallelization while keeping the `unstructured` executable name.

- [unstructured.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/unstructured_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_cuda_r2/unstructured/unstructured.cpp:142) now uses a coalesced GPU structure-of-arrays kernel, double buffering, and a CUDA Graph for iteration launches.
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/unstructured_gpt-5.6-terra-xhigh_cuda_r2/unstructured/CMakeLists.txt:1) unconditionally builds with CUDA for Ampere (`sm_86`), with no CPU simulation fallback.

Verified:
- 16×16 / 10 and 32×32 / 3 hashes exactly match the original.
- Default 512×512 / 10 validates successfully, running at about 147 GFLOPS on this RTX 3090.

+namespace {

+constexpr unsigned int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+[[noreturn]] void cudaCheckFailed(cudaError_t status, const char* expression,
+                                  const char* file, int line) {
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while executing %s: %s\n", file, line,
+            expression, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression)                                                      \
+    do {                                                                            \
+        const cudaError_t cuda_status_ = (expression);                              \
+        if (cuda_status_ != cudaSuccess) {                                          \
+            cudaCheckFailed(cuda_status_, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__);         \
+        }                                                                           \
+    } while (false)
+
+// Metadata is transposed before upload: connection k for adjacent elements is
+// adjacent in memory.  This keeps every connection pass through a warp fully
+// coalesced, while retaining the original arbitrary adjacency-list semantics.
+// The mesh builder uses int element indices and the three fixed material IDs,
+// so their compact device representations are lossless for every valid input.
+__global__ void updateElementsKernel(
+    size_t n_elems,
+    const uint8_t* __restrict__ material_indices,
+    const uint8_t* __restrict__ connection_counts,
+    const uint32_t* __restrict__ connection_indices,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ connection_fluxes,
+    const Material* __restrict__ materials,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ read_energy,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ read_total_flux,
+    val_t* __restrict__ write_energy,
+    val_t* __restrict__ write_total_flux) {
+    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n_elems) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const Material material = materials[material_indices[i]];
+    const val_t current_energy = read_energy[i];
+    val_t total_flux = material.external_flow;
+    const uint8_t connection_count = connection_counts[i];
+
+    // MAX_CONNECTIONS is a compile-time constant.  Predicated, unrolled loads
+    // avoid loop-control overhead without accessing inactive graph entries.
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {
+        if (j < connection_count) {
+            const size_t connection_offset = static_cast<size_t>(j) * n_elems + i;
+            const uint32_t neighbor_idx = connection_indices[connection_offset];
+            const val_t neighbor_energy = read_energy[neighbor_idx];
+            total_flux += (neighbor_energy - current_energy) * material.transfer_coeff *
+                          connection_fluxes[connection_offset] * 0.25;
+        }
+    }
+
+    write_energy[i] = current_energy + total_flux;
+    write_total_flux[i] = read_total_flux[i] + fabs(total_flux);
+}
+

+    std::vector<val_t> initial_total_flux(gpu.n_elems);
+

+        material_indices[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(source_static.material_idx);
+        connection_counts[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(source_static.num_connections);
+        initial_energy[i] = source_dynamic.current_energy;
+        initial_total_flux[i] = source_dynamic.total_flux;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {
+            const size_t connection_offset = j * gpu.n_elems + i;
+            connection_indices[connection_offset] =
+                static_cast<uint32_t>(source_static.connected_idx[j]);
+            connection_fluxes[connection_offset] = source_static.connected_flux[j];
+        }
+    }
+
+    allocateDevice(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&gpu.material_indices),
+                   gpu.n_elems * sizeof(*gpu.material_indices));
+    allocateDevice(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&gpu.connection_counts),
+                   gpu.n_elems * sizeof(*gpu.connection_counts));
+    allocateDevice(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&gpu.connection_indices),
+                   connection_indices.size() * sizeof(*gpu.connection_indices));
+    allocateDevice(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&gpu.connection_fluxes),
+                   connection_fluxes.size() * sizeof(*gpu.connection_fluxes));
+    allocateDevice(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&gpu.materials),
+                   world.materials.size() * sizeof(*gpu.materials));
+    for (int buffer = 0; buffer < 2; ++buffer) {
+        allocateDevice(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&gpu.energy[buffer]),
+                       gpu.n_elems * sizeof(*gpu.energy[buffer]));
+        allocateDevice(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&gpu.total_flux[buffer]),
+                       gpu.n_elems * sizeof(*gpu.total_flux[buffer]));
+    }
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(gpu.material_indices, material_indices.data(),
+                          material_indices.size() * sizeof(*gpu.material_indices),
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(gpu.connection_counts, connection_counts.data(),
+                          connection_counts.size() * sizeof(*gpu.connection_counts),
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(gpu.connection_indices, connection_indices.data(),
+                          connection_indices.size() * sizeof(*gpu.connection_indices),
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(gpu.connection_fluxes, connection_fluxes.data(),
+                          connection_fluxes.size() * sizeof(*gpu.connection_fluxes),
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(gpu.materials, world.materials.data(),
+                          world.materials.size() * sizeof(*gpu.materials),
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(gpu.energy[0], initial_energy.data(),
+                          initial_energy.size() * sizeof(*gpu.energy[0]),
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(gpu.total_flux[0], initial_total_flux.data(),
+                          initial_total_flux.size() * sizeof(*gpu.total_flux[0]),
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+
+    buildExecutionGraph(gpu, n_iters);
+    return gpu;
+}
+
+void runSimulation(GpuSimulation& gpu) {
+    if (gpu.graph_exec != nullptr) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphLaunch(gpu.graph_exec, nullptr));
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+}
+
+void downloadSimulation(World& world, const GpuSimulation& gpu) {
+    if (gpu.n_elems == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    char* energy_destination = reinterpret_cast<char*>(world.elements_dynamic.data()) +
+                               offsetof(ElementDynamic, current_energy);
+    char* flux_destination = reinterpret_cast<char*>(world.elements_dynamic.data()) +
+                             offsetof(ElementDynamic, total_flux);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(energy_destination, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+                            gpu.energy[gpu.final_buffer], sizeof(val_t), sizeof(val_t), gpu.n_elems,
+                            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(flux_destination, sizeof(ElementDynamic),
+                            gpu.total_flux[gpu.final_buffer], sizeof(val_t), sizeof(val_t), gpu.n_elems,
+                            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+}
+
+void releaseSimulation(GpuSimulation& gpu) {
+    if (gpu.graph_exec != nullptr) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphExecDestroy(gpu.graph_exec));
+    }
+    if (gpu.graph != nullptr) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGraphDestroy(gpu.graph));
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.material_indices));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.connection_counts));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.connection_indices));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.connection_fluxes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.materials));
+    for (int buffer = 0; buffer < 2; ++buffer) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.energy[buffer]));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(gpu.total_flux[buffer]));
+    }
+}
+
+}  // namespace
+

 bool validateResults(const World& world) {
     val_t energy_sum = 0.0;
